RESERVE BANK OF INDIA 



NOTIFICATION 

Calcutta, the 4th September 1954. 

In pursuance of Rule 18 of the Rules made by the Government of India under Seotion 28 of the Public Debt Act 1944 and 
published in the Gazette of In li& of the 20th April 1946 the followin'* list is hereby advertised of securities lost eto. in respect 
of which prima facie grounds exist for believing that the securities have been lost and that the claim of the applicants is just. 
All parsons other thm respective claiments named below who have any claim, upon these securities, should communicate 
im nediately with the Manager, Reserve Bank of India, Calcutta. 

This list is divided into two parts Part ‘ A ’ being the list of the securities now advertised for the first time 
and Part 1 B ’ the list of securities previously advertised.

NOTICE 

Calcutta, the 27 th August 1954 
The Parliament House Pay Office, New Delhi (under 
New Delhi Branch), which was closed for the recess at 
the close of business on the 28th May 1954, has been re- 
opened on the 19th July 1954, under the charge of Mr, 
P. N. Relan, Staff Assistant.

INDIAN COAL GRADING BOARD 


Corrigendum to Annual Report for the year 1953-54 

In the above Report published in the Gazette of India 
dated the 7th August 1954 the item “Receipts during the 
vear. under Section 4(2) Note ‘ B ’ ” should be read 
Rs. 180 and not Rs. 1,800 which is a printing mistake. 

A. T. MUKHERJEE A B. GUHA 

Secretary President
